- **hw-integrate**: Hands every ruled pull request of the Headwater build order to the merge queue, waits for each to land or be ejected, then moves the shared checkout, rebuilds and regenerates once, and writes back to the board. ([headwater-ai/headwater](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hw-integrate.agent.md))
- **hw-integrate**: Hands every ruled pull request of the Headwater build order to the merge queue, waits for each to land or be ejected, and writes back to the board. - **hw-iterate**: Owns the build, verify and rework loop for one adjudicated issue of the Headwater build order, so that the parent hears once per issue. Use in place of dispatching hw-build and hw-verify directly, after hw-adjudicate returns BUILD and the footprint is claimed. It dispatches the builder and a fresh verifier each in its own worktree, sends each FAIL back to the same builder with the finding verbatim, and stops at the third FAIL. - **hw-junior-pcb-engineer**: Junior PCB Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Reviews against checklists/junior.md with cited evidence and writes findings/junior.json. Read-only. - **hw-manufacturing-engineer**: Manufacturing Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Owns triage of every automatic-check FAIL/WARN. Reviews against checklists/manufacturing.md with cited evidence and writes findings/manufacturing.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-manufacturing-engineer.md))
- **hw-mechanical-engineer**: Mechanical Integration Engineer on the /pcb-review team. Reviews against checklists/mechanical.md with cited evidence and writes findings/mechanical.json. Read-only. ([7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team](https://github.com/7mudit14-svg/pcb-review-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hw-mechanical-engineer.md))

- **hw-power-engineer**: Power Engineer on the /pcb-review team. - **hw-verify**: Attacks one branch of the Headwater build order adversarially and returns a verdict the parent rules on. Use as the third stage of an iteration, after hw-build has opened the pull request. It resets a scratch worktree to the branch, runs the suite and the attacks the parent chose from the verification bar, waits on the pull request itself, and edits nothing. ([headwater-ai/headwater](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hw-verify.agent.md))
- **hw-verify**: Verifies CoCo 3 / MC6809 / GIME hardware claims against the authoritative local PDFs in the reference checkout, the SEB Unravelled II ROM disassembly, the real ROM bytes in ~/.local/share/cocovm/assets/roms, and the local MAME clone at ~/code/mame. Use BEFORE implementing anything that hinges on register semantics, bit layouts, timing, or ROM behavior. Read-only; returns cited findings, never edits code. - **hwang-si-mok**: The dispassionate analyst — an incorruptible second opinion. Use to evaluate a claim, plan, proposal, argument, or decision on pure evidence and logic, stripped of hype, spin, sunk cost, authority, and emotional framing. Catches the inconsistency and the manipulation precisely because rhetoric doesn't move him. No flattery, no fear, no favor. Pairs with han-yeo-jin, who supplies the human read he cannot feel. ([takoyaro/the-agency](https://github.com/takoyaro/the-agency/blob/HEAD/plugins/hwang-si-mok/agents/hwang-si-mok.md) · ★1)
- **hwm-planner-agent**: Hierarchical Planning with Latent World Models (HWM). - **hybridizer-porter**: Analyses a candidate C# function and proposes a Hybridizer kernel shape — parallelism layout (Parallel.For vs one-block-per-row), data lifetime (marshalled vs resident), atomics strategy, per-flavor split decision, and the gotchas to watch. Returns a structured proposal, NOT code. Use this before writing a kernel, not as a substitute for writing it. ([hybridizer-io/claude-plugin](https://github.com/hybridizer-io/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/hybridizer-port/agents/hybridizer-porter.md))
- **hybridizer-reviewer**: Reviews a candidate Hybridizer kernel — pending changes, a specific file, or selected lines — against the known gotchas list before the user spends time compiling. Returns a structured findings report. Catches MathF use, AtomicExpr.apply, OMP wrapper races, FloatResidentArray init bugs, missing [In]/[Out], stale-satellite traps, and similar issues. ([hybridizer-io/claude-plugin](https://github.com/hybridizer-io/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/hybridizer-port/agents/hybridizer-reviewer.md))
